候選、失敗與保全

7 個模組候選；12 個 Feature trace；3595 個來源定址的既有 fixture 列。Outcome token 不自動推論完整前後狀態；缺欄位明列 OPEN。符號 fixture 的資料保全不是原生 IO／durability 證據。

M01

CR-D1..D3: native lease issuer; durable Ready/restart issuance; closed publish representation admission; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M02

R09-R11 representation/error admission; native activation fence/provider; golden14 and projector/lock proof; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M03

H2 D1-D8 and C1 adoption; exact String denial/legacy entry no-IO boundary; M04 persistence provider; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M04

G1-G4 save Held/root/ACK observation mapping; full S2/CX codec/envelope adoption; authentic issuer/fence; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M05

H1-H4 full request mapping; native issuer/fence/inclusion; response mapping; uncertain CAS reconciliation; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M06

MH1-MH6 Merge mutation bridge; native issuer/root registration/Undo pin; postcommit semantics; typed ESS projector; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

M07

RA-L1 ordinal formula choice; PH-G2/G3/G4 issuer/root/restart; RA-L2 replay/baseline atomicity; RA-L3 provider fixture limitation; exact adopted file envelope, compilable intended RED and separate acceptance before standing implementation authority activates.

跨決策與驗證界線

G02／G03／G04 仍各自按原 scope 製備，不作 G01 先完成的依賴。所有權、issuer、transaction、error admission、migration 與 replay 衝突未自動解決。M08 corrected admission 是文件審查，非 Owner 採納。

產品測試、有效 RED、build、package、install、native runtime、release：NOT RUN。作者僅做結構與來源檢查；獨立 reviewer 才能出文件驗收。
